2025-11-28T17:06:38.639475285Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:06:38.639297Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT status, COUNT(*)::bigint AS …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT status, COUNT(*)::bigint AS count\n        FROM video_generation_jobs\n        GROUP BY status\n        \n","rows_affected":1,"rows_returned":1,"elapsed":"848.06µs","elapsed_secs":0.00084806},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:06:38.641110053Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:06:38.640993Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT COUNT(*)::bigint AS count …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT COUNT(*)::bigint AS count\n        FROM video_generation_jobs\n        WHERE status = 'failed'\n          AND updated_at >= NOW() - INTERVAL '24 hours'\n        \n","rows_affected":1,"rows_returned":1,"elapsed":"783.048µs","elapsed_secs":0.000783048},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:06:38.642615227Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:06:38.642524Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT COUNT(*)::bigint AS count …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT COUNT(*)::bigint AS count\n        FROM video_generation_jobs\n        WHERE status = 'completed'\n          AND updated_at >= NOW() - INTERVAL '24 hours'\n        \n","rows_affected":1,"rows_returned":1,"elapsed":"679.715µs","elapsed_secs":0.000679715},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:06:38.644191534Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:06:38.644086Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT MAX(updated_at) AS last_completed …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT MAX(updated_at) AS last_completed\n        FROM video_generation_jobs\n        WHERE status = 'completed'\n        \n","rows_affected":1,"rows_returned":1,"elapsed":"811.748µs","elapsed_secs":0.000811748},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:06:38.645689458Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:06:38.645604Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT job_id, status, updated_at …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT job_id, status, updated_at\n        FROM video_generation_jobs\n        WHERE status IN ('queued', 'running')\n          AND updated_at < NOW() - INTERVAL '30 minutes'\n        ORDER BY updated_at ASC\n        LIMIT 10\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"728.046µs","elapsed_secs":0.000728046},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:06:38.647389417Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:06:38.647302Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT COUNT(*) AS total …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT COUNT(*) AS total\n        FROM media\n        WHERE media_type = 'video'\n          AND uploaded_at >= NOW() - ($1::int * INTERVAL '1 day')\n        \n","rows_affected":1,"rows_returned":1,"elapsed":"944.431µs","elapsed_secs":0.000944431},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:06:38.650039329Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:06:38.649058Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT\n            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE event_type = 'view')   AS views,\n            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE event_type = 'share')  AS shares,\n            COALESCE(AVG((metadata ->> 'quality_score')::float), 0.0) AS avg_quality\n        FROM media_engagement\n        WHERE occurred_at >= NOW() - ($1::int * INTERVAL '1 day')\n          AND event_type IN ('view', 'share', 'quality_score')\n        \n","rows_affected":1,"rows_returned":1,"elapsed":"892.211µs","elapsed_secs":0.000892211},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:06:38.650701964Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:06:38.650624Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT\n            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE status = 'completed')                       AS completed,\n            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE status IN ('scheduled', 'processing'))      AS pending\n        FROM media_distribution\n        WHERE updated_at >= NOW() - ($1::int * INTERVAL '1 day')\n        \n","rows_affected":1,"rows_returned":1,"elapsed":"789.058µs","elapsed_secs":0.000789058},"target":"sqlx::query"}

2025-11-28T17:06:49.277432204Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:06:49.277350Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT lfs.id, lfs.live_session_id, lfs.service_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT\n                lfs.id,\n                lfs.live_session_id,\n                lfs.service_id,\n                lfs.promo_price_cfa,\n                ls.host_user_id,\n                ls.service_id AS primary_service_id,\n                ls.metadata,\n                ls.title\n            FROM live_flash_sales lfs\n            JOIN live_sessions ls ON ls.id = lfs.live_session_id\n            WHERE lfs.status = 'scheduled'\n              AND lfs.start_at <= $1\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"2.030017ms","elapsed_secs":0.002030017},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:06:49.277564537Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:06:49.277484Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT e.id AS entry_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT\n            e.id AS entry_id,\n            e.service_id,\n            e.submitted_by_user_id,\n            ev.display_name AS event_display_name\n        FROM global_promo_entries e\n        JOIN global_promo_events ev ON ev.id = e.event_id\n        WHERE ev.status = 'live'\n          AND e.status = 'approved'\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"1.616217ms","elapsed_secs":0.001616217},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:06:49.279021921Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:06:49.278935Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT lfs.id, lfs.live_session_id, lfs.service_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT\n                lfs.id,\n                lfs.live_session_id,\n                lfs.service_id,\n                lfs.end_at,\n                lfs.ending_notification_sent_at,\n                ls.host_user_id,\n                ls.service_id AS primary_service_id,\n                ls.metadata,\n                ls.title\n            FROM live_flash_sales lfs\n            JOIN live_sessions ls ON ls.id = lfs.live_session_id\n            WHERE lfs.status = 'live'\n              AND lfs.end_at <= $1\n              AND lfs.ending_notification_sent_at IS NULL\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"925.101µs","elapsed_secs":0.000925101},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:06:49.280356002Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:06:49.280254Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT e.id AS entry_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT\n            e.id AS entry_id,\n            e.service_id,\n            e.submitted_by_user_id,\n            ev.display_name AS event_display_name\n        FROM global_promo_entries e\n        JOIN global_promo_events ev ON ev.id = e.event_id\n        WHERE ev.status = 'archived'\n          AND e.status <> 'ended'\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"2.085738ms","elapsed_secs":0.002085738},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:06:49.280898624Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:06:49.280807Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"WITH reservations AS ( …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            WITH reservations AS (\n                SELECT flash_sale_id, SUM(quantity) AS reserved_quantity\n                FROM live_flash_sale_reservations\n                GROUP BY flash_sale_id\n            )\n            SELECT\n                lfs.id,\n                lfs.live_session_id,\n                lfs.service_id,\n                lfs.stock_target,\n                COALESCE(res.reserved_quantity, 0) AS reserved_quantity,\n                lfs.commentary_interval_seconds,\n                lfs.last_commentary_sent_at,\n                lfs.end_at,\n                COALESCE(lfs.metadata, '{}'::jsonb) AS flash_metadata,\n                ls.host_user_id,\n                ls.service_id AS primary_service_id,\n                COALESCE(ls.metadata, '{}'::jsonb) AS session_metadata,\n                ls.title\n            FROM live_flash_sales lfs\n            JOIN live_sessions ls ON ls.id = lfs.live_session_id\n            LEFT JOIN reservations res ON res.flash_sale_id = lfs.id\n            WHERE lfs.status = 'live'\n              AND lfs.commentary_mode = 'ai_voice'\n              AND lfs.end_at > $1\n              AND (\n                    lfs.last_commentary_sent_at IS NULL\n                    OR lfs.last_commentary_sent_at <= $1 - make_interval(secs => lfs.commentary_interval_seconds)\n              )\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"1.159907ms","elapsed_secs":0.001159907},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:06:49.282352907Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:06:49.282275Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"UPDATE live_flash_sales SET status …","db.statement":"\n\nUPDATE live_flash_sales SET status = 'ended', updated_at = NOW() WHERE status IN ('scheduled', 'live') AND end_at <= $1\n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"778.187µs","elapsed_secs":0.000778187},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:06:49.384292945Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:06:49.384151Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT id, delivery_id, zone_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT\n                id,\n                delivery_id,\n                zone_id,\n                status,\n                priority,\n                attempt_count,\n                payload,\n                next_attempt_at,\n                enqueued_at,\n                updated_at\n            FROM delivery_matching_queue\n            WHERE status IN ('queued', 'searching')\n              AND next_attempt_at <= NOW()\n            ORDER BY priority ASC, next_attempt_at ASC\n            LIMIT $1\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"927.441µs","elapsed_secs":0.000927441},"target":"sqlx::query"}

2025-11-28T17:06:53.746399125Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:06:53.746313Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT job_id, status, updated_at …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT job_id, status, updated_at\n        FROM video_generation_jobs\n        WHERE status IN ('queued', 'running')\n          AND updated_at < NOW() - INTERVAL '30 minutes'\n        ORDER BY updated_at ASC\n        LIMIT 10\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"654.625µs","elapsed_secs":0.000654625},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:06:53.748081273Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:06:53.747939Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT COUNT(*) AS total …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT COUNT(*) AS total\n        FROM media\n        WHERE media_type = 'video'\n          AND uploaded_at >= NOW() - ($1::int * INTERVAL '1 day')\n        \n","rows_affected":1,"rows_returned":1,"elapsed":"887.62µs","elapsed_secs":0.00088762},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:06:53.749628029Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:06:53.749526Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT\n            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE event_type = 'view')   AS views,\n            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE event_type = 'share')  AS shares,\n            COALESCE(AVG((metadata ->> 'quality_score')::float), 0.0) AS avg_quality\n        FROM media_engagement\n        WHERE occurred_at >= NOW() - ($1::int * INTERVAL '1 day')\n          AND event_type IN ('view', 'share', 'quality_score')\n        \n","rows_affected":1,"rows_returned":1,"elapsed":"779.488µs","elapsed_secs":0.000779488},"target":"sqlx::query"}

2025-11-28T17:07:07.79917703Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:07:07.798363Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT *, COALESCE( calculate_location_score($25::TEXT, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT \n            *,\n            COALESCE(\n                calculate_location_score($25::TEXT, location_vector, chosen_location), \n                0.0\n            ) as location_score,\n            (usage_count::FLOAT / 100.0) as popularity_score\n        FROM autocomplete_combinations\n        WHERE EXISTS (\n                SELECT 1 FROM unnest(full_vector) AS elem \n                WHERE LOWER(elem) LIKE '%' || $1 || '%'\n            ) AND EXISTS (\n                SELECT 1 FROM unnest(full_vector) AS elem \n                WHERE LOWER(elem) LIKE '%' || $2 || '%'\n            ) AND 2025-11-28T17:07:08.297170538Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:07:08.296887Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT *, COALESCE( calculate_location_score($3::TEXT, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT \n            *,\n            COALESCE(\n                calculate_location_score($3::TEXT, location_vector, chosen_location), \n                0.0\n            ) as location_score,\n            (usage_count::FLOAT / 100.0) as popularity_score\n        FROM autocomplete_combinations\n        WHERE EXISTS (\n                SELECT 1 FROM unnest(full_vector) AS elem \n                WHERE LOWER(elem) LIKE '%' || $1 || '%'\n            ) AND EXISTS (\n                SELECT 1 FROM unnest(full_vector) AS elem \n                WHERE LOWER(elem) LIKE '%' || $2 || '%'\n            )\n        ORDER BY \n            is_ai_preferred DESC,\n            (COALESCE(calculate_location_score($3::TEXT, location_vector, chosen_location), 0.0) * 0.7 \n             + (usage_count::FLOAT / 100.0) * 0.3) DESC\n        LIMIT $4\n        \n","rows_affected":8,"rows_returned":8,"elapsed":"799.388158ms","elapsed_secs":0.799388158},"target":"sqlx::query"}

2025-11-28T17:07:08.603660726Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:07:08.603514Z","level":"WARN","fields":{"message":"slow statement: execution time exceeded alert threshold","summary":"WITH recent_trend AS ( …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        WITH recent_trend AS (\n            SELECT \n                ac.id,\n                ac.product_vector,\n                ac.usage_count as current_usage,\n                -- Calcul tendance : usage_count actuel vs il y a 7 jours (via updated_at)\n                CASE \n                    WHEN ac.updated_at >= NOW() - INTERVAL '7 days' \n                    THEN 1 -- Tendance hausse (produit actif récemment)\n                    ELSE 0 -- Stable/baisse\n                END as is_trending\n            FROM autocomplete_combinations ac\n            WHERE ac.usage_count >= 2\n        )\n        SELECT \n            ac.product_vector,\n            ac.product_labels,\n            ac.usage_count,\n            ac.prix::FLOAT8 AS prix_float,\n            ac.has_variant,\n            ac.variant_dimension,\n            ac.variant_value,\n            rt.is_trending\n        FROM autocomplete_combinations ac\n        INNER JOIN recent_trend rt ON rt.id = ac.id\n        WHERE \n            -- Recherche dans le vecteur produit (ANY permet de chercher dans l'array)\n            EXISTS (\n                SELECT 1 FROM unnest(ac.product_vector) AS val\n                WHERE val ILIKE '%' || $1 || '%'\n            )\n            -- Seulement les produits avec usage_count >= 2 (vraiment populaires)\n            AND ac.usage_count >= 2\n        ORDER BY \n            rt.is_trending DESC,  -- Tendances EN PREMIER\n            ac.usage_count DESC,  -- Puis popularité\n            ac.prix::FLOAT8 ASC           -- Puis prix\n        LIMIT $2\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"1.260685551s","elapsed_secs":1.260685551,"slow_threshold":"1s"},"target":"sqlx::query"}

2025-11-28T17:07:09.399485912Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:07:09.399340Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT *, COALESCE( calculate_location_score($3::TEXT, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT \n            *,\n            COALESCE(\n                calculate_location_score($3::TEXT, location_vector, chosen_location), \n                0.0\n            ) as location_score,\n            (usage_count::FLOAT / 100.0) as popularity_score\n        FROM autocomplete_combinations\n        WHERE EXISTS (\n                SELECT 1 FROM unnest(full_vector) AS elem \n                WHERE LOWER(elem) LIKE '%' || $1 || '%'\n            ) AND EXISTS (\n                SELECT 1 FROM unnest(full_vector) AS elem \n                WHERE LOWER(elem) LIKE '%' || $2 || '%'\n            )\n        ORDER BY \n            is_ai_preferred DESC,\n            (COALESCE(calculate_location_score($3::TEXT, location_vector, chosen_location), 0.0) * 0.7 \n             + (usage_count::FLOAT / 100.0) * 0.3) DESC\n        LIMIT $4\n        \n","rows_affected":8,"rows_returned":8,"elapsed":"257.698164ms","elapsed_secs":0.257698164},"target":"sqlx::query"}

2025-11-28T17:07:19.27478636Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:07:19.274691Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT lfs.id, lfs.live_session_id, lfs.service_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT\n                lfs.id,\n                lfs.live_session_id,\n                lfs.service_id,\n                lfs.promo_price_cfa,\n                lfs.start_at,\n                lfs.scheduled_notification_sent_at,\n                ls.host_user_id,\n                ls.service_id AS primary_service_id,\n                ls.metadata,\n                ls.title\n            FROM live_flash_sales lfs\n            JOIN live_sessions ls ON ls.id = lfs.live_session_id\n            WHERE lfs.status = 'scheduled'\n              AND lfs.start_at <= $1\n              AND lfs.scheduled_notification_sent_at IS NULL\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"1.848942ms","elapsed_secs":0.001848942},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:07:19.275108357Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:07:19.275038Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT id, display_name FROM …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT id, display_name\n        FROM global_promo_events\n        WHERE status IN ('scheduled','live') AND ends_at <= $1\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"754.057µs","elapsed_secs":0.000754057},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:07:19.276344786Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:07:19.276255Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT lfs.id, lfs.live_session_id, lfs.service_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT\n                lfs.id,\n                lfs.live_session_id,\n                lfs.service_id,\n                lfs.promo_price_cfa,\n                ls.host_user_id,\n                ls.service_id AS primary_service_id,\n                ls.metadata,\n                ls.title\n            FROM live_flash_sales lfs\n            JOIN live_sessions ls ON ls.id = lfs.live_session_id\n            WHERE lfs.status = 'scheduled'\n              AND lfs.start_at <= $1\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"906.541µs","elapsed_secs":0.000906541},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:07:19.277333348Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:07:19.277261Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT e.id AS entry_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT\n            e.id AS entry_id,\n            e.service_id,\n            e.submitted_by_user_id,\n            ev.display_name AS event_display_name\n        FROM global_promo_entries e\n        JOIN global_promo_events ev ON ev.id = e.event_id\n        WHERE ev.status = 'live'\n          AND e.status = 'approved'\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"1.566357ms","elapsed_secs":0.001566357},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:07:19.278616448Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:07:19.278530Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT lfs.id, lfs.live_session_id, lfs.service_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT\n                lfs.id,\n                lfs.live_session_id,\n                lfs.service_id,\n                lfs.end_at,\n                lfs.ending_notification_sent_at,\n                ls.host_user_id,\n                ls.service_id AS primary_service_id,\n                ls.metadata,\n                ls.title\n            FROM live_flash_sales lfs\n            JOIN live_sessions ls ON ls.id = lfs.live_session_id\n            WHERE lfs.status = 'live'\n              AND lfs.end_at <= $1\n              AND lfs.ending_notification_sent_at IS NULL\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"1.630318ms","elapsed_secs":0.001630318},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:07:19.278743101Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:07:19.278662Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT e.id AS entry_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT\n            e.id AS entry_id,\n            e.service_id,\n            e.submitted_by_user_id,\n            ev.display_name AS event_display_name\n        FROM global_promo_entries e\n        JOIN global_promo_events ev ON ev.id = e.event_id\n        WHERE ev.status = 'archived'\n          AND e.status <> 'ended'\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"748.437µs","elapsed_secs":0.000748437},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:07:19.281623437Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:07:19.281543Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"WITH reservations AS ( …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            WITH reservations AS (\n                SELECT flash_sale_id, SUM(quantity) AS reserved_quantity\n                FROM live_flash_sale_reservations\n                GROUP BY flash_sale_id\n            )\n            SELECT\n                lfs.id,\n                lfs.live_session_id,\n                lfs.service_id,\n                lfs.stock_target,\n                COALESCE(res.reserved_quantity, 0) AS reserved_quantity,\n                lfs.commentary_interval_seconds,\n                lfs.last_commentary_sent_at,\n                lfs.end_at,\n                COALESCE(lfs.metadata, '{}'::jsonb) AS flash_metadata,\n                ls.host_user_id,\n                ls.service_id AS primary_service_id,\n                COALESCE(ls.metadata, '{}'::jsonb) AS session_metadata,\n                ls.title\n            FROM live_flash_sales lfs\n            JOIN live_sessions ls ON ls.id = lfs.live_session_id\n            LEFT JOIN reservations res ON res.flash_sale_id = lfs.id\n            WHERE lfs.status = 'live'\n              AND lfs.commentary_mode = 'ai_voice'\n              AND lfs.end_at > $1\n              AND (\n                    lfs.last_commentary_sent_at IS NULL\n                    OR lfs.last_commentary_sent_at <= $1 - make_interval(secs => lfs.commentary_interval_seconds)\n              )\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"2.363614ms","elapsed_secs":0.002363614},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:07:19.283102471Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:07:19.283015Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"UPDATE live_flash_sales SET status …","db.statement":"\n\nUPDATE live_flash_sales SET status = 'ended', updated_at = NOW() WHERE status IN ('scheduled', 'live') AND end_at <= $1\n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"816.589µs","elapsed_secs":0.000816589},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:07:19.382727665Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:07:19.382586Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT id, delivery_id, zone_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT\n                id,\n                delivery_id,\n                zone_id,\n                status,\n                priority,\n                attempt_count,\n                payload,\n                next_attempt_at,\n                enqueued_at,\n                updated_at\n            FROM delivery_matching_queue\n            WHERE status IN ('queued', 'searching')\n              AND next_attempt_at <= NOW()\n            ORDER BY priority ASC, next_attempt_at ASC\n            LIMIT $1\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"908.221µs","elapsed_secs":0.000908221},"target":"sqlx::query"}

2025-11-28T17:07:19.470377344Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:07:19.470237Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT id, media_id, platform, …","db.statement":"\n\nSELECT id,\n                 media_id,\n                 platform,\n                 payload,\n                 status,\n                 attempt,\n                 last_error,\n                 scheduled_for,\n                 created_at,\n                 updated_at\n         FROM social_publication_jobs\n         WHERE status = 'queued' AND scheduled_for <= NOW()\n         ORDER BY scheduled_for ASC\n         LIMIT $1\n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"1.902873ms","elapsed_secs":0.001902873},"target":"sqlx::query"}

2025-11-28T17:07:19.488180794Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:07:19.488100Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT d.id, d.status::text as …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT \n                d.id,\n                d.status::text as status,\n                d.updated_at,\n                d.creator_id,\n                d.recipient_user_id,\n                c.user_id as courier_user_id\n            FROM deliveries d\n            LEFT JOIN couriers c ON c.id = d.courier_id\n            WHERE \n                d.status::text = $1\n                AND d.updated_at < NOW() - ($2 || ' minutes')::interval\n                AND d.status::text != 'delivered'\n                AND d.status::text != 'cancelled'\n                AND d.status::text != 'completed'\n            LIMIT 20\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"751.438µs","elapsed_secs":0.000751438},"target":"sqlx::query"}

2025-11-28T17:07:38.648038357Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:07:38.647908Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT\n            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE event_type = 'view')   AS views,\n            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE event_type = 'share')  AS shares,\n            COALESCE(AVG((metadata ->> 'quality_score')::float), 0.0) AS avg_quality\n        FROM media_engagement\n        WHERE occurred_at >= NOW() - ($1::int * INTERVAL '1 day')\n          AND event_type IN ('view', 'share', 'quality_score')\n        \n","rows_affected":1,"rows_returned":1,"elapsed":"807.669µs","elapsed_secs":0.000807669},"target":"sqlx::query"}

2025-11-28T17:07:49.274130055Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:07:49.274027Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT lfs.id, lfs.live_session_id, lfs.service_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT\n                lfs.id,\n                lfs.live_session_id,\n                lfs.service_id,\n                lfs.promo_price_cfa,\n                lfs.start_at,\n                lfs.scheduled_notification_sent_at,\n                ls.host_user_id,\n                ls.service_id AS primary_service_id,\n                ls.metadata,\n                ls.title\n            FROM live_flash_sales lfs\n            JOIN live_sessions ls ON ls.id = lfs.live_session_id\n            WHERE lfs.status = 'scheduled'\n              AND lfs.start_at <= $1\n              AND lfs.scheduled_notification_sent_at IS NULL\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"1.017484ms","elapsed_secs":0.001017484},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:07:49.274568834Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:07:49.274481Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT id, display_name FROM …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT id, display_name\n        FROM global_promo_events\n        WHERE status = 'scheduled' AND starts_at <= $1\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"1.565507ms","elapsed_secs":0.001565507},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:07:49.275795913Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:07:49.275699Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT lfs.id, lfs.live_session_id, lfs.service_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT\n                lfs.id,\n                lfs.live_session_id,\n                lfs.service_id,\n                lfs.promo_price_cfa,\n                ls.host_user_id,\n                ls.service_id AS primary_service_id,\n                ls.metadata,\n                ls.title\n            FROM live_flash_sales lfs\n            JOIN live_sessions ls ON ls.id = lfs.live_session_id\n            WHERE lfs.status = 'scheduled'\n              AND lfs.start_at <= $1\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"906.041µs","elapsed_secs":0.000906041},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:07:49.276005768Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:07:49.275892Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT id, display_name FROM …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT id, display_name\n        FROM global_promo_events\n        WHERE status IN ('scheduled','live') AND ends_at <= $1\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"739.497µs","elapsed_secs":0.000739497},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:07:49.277291277Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:07:49.277206Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT e.id AS entry_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT\n            e.id AS entry_id,\n            e.service_id,\n            e.submitted_by_user_id,\n            ev.display_name AS event_display_name\n        FROM global_promo_entries e\n        JOIN global_promo_events ev ON ev.id = e.event_id\n        WHERE ev.status = 'live'\n          AND e.status = 'approved'\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"636.134µs","elapsed_secs":0.000636134},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:07:49.27739999Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:07:49.277335Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT lfs.id, lfs.live_session_id, lfs.service_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT\n                lfs.id,\n                lfs.live_session_id,\n                lfs.service_id,\n                lfs.end_at,\n                lfs.ending_notification_sent_at,\n                ls.host_user_id,\n                ls.service_id AS primary_service_id,\n                ls.metadata,\n                ls.title\n            FROM live_flash_sales lfs\n            JOIN live_sessions ls ON ls.id = lfs.live_session_id\n            WHERE lfs.status = 'live'\n              AND lfs.end_at <= $1\n              AND lfs.ending_notification_sent_at IS NULL\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"929.071µs","elapsed_secs":0.000929071},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:07:49.278623408Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:07:49.278541Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT e.id AS entry_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT\n            e.id AS entry_id,\n            e.service_id,\n            e.submitted_by_user_id,\n            ev.display_name AS event_display_name\n        FROM global_promo_entries e\n        JOIN global_promo_events ev ON ev.id = e.event_id\n        WHERE ev.status = 'archived'\n          AND e.status <> 'ended'\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"658.395µs","elapsed_secs":0.000658395},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:07:49.279067298Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:07:49.278983Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"WITH reservations AS ( …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            WITH reservations AS (\n                SELECT flash_sale_id, SUM(quantity) AS reserved_quantity\n                FROM live_flash_sale_reservations\n                GROUP BY flash_sale_id\n            )\n            SELECT\n                lfs.id,\n                lfs.live_session_id,\n                lfs.service_id,\n                lfs.stock_target,\n                COALESCE(res.reserved_quantity, 0) AS reserved_quantity,\n                lfs.commentary_interval_seconds,\n                lfs.last_commentary_sent_at,\n                lfs.end_at,\n                COALESCE(lfs.metadata, '{}'::jsonb) AS flash_metadata,\n                ls.host_user_id,\n                ls.service_id AS primary_service_id,\n                COALESCE(ls.metadata, '{}'::jsonb) AS session_metadata,\n                ls.title\n            FROM live_flash_sales lfs\n            JOIN live_sessions ls ON ls.id = lfs.live_session_id\n            LEFT JOIN reservations res ON res.flash_sale_id = lfs.id\n            WHERE lfs.status = 'live'\n              AND lfs.commentary_mode = 'ai_voice'\n              AND lfs.end_at > $1\n              AND (\n                    lfs.last_commentary_sent_at IS NULL\n                    OR lfs.last_commentary_sent_at <= $1 - make_interval(secs => lfs.commentary_interval_seconds)\n              )\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"1.022093ms","elapsed_secs":0.001022093},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:07:49.280582133Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:07:49.280488Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"UPDATE live_flash_sales SET status …","db.statement":"\n\nUPDATE live_flash_sales SET status = 'ended', updated_at = NOW() WHERE status IN ('scheduled', 'live') AND end_at <= $1\n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"805.719µs","elapsed_secs":0.000805719},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:07:49.383277008Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:07:49.383110Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT id, delivery_id, zone_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT\n                id,\n                delivery_id,\n                zone_id,\n                status,\n                priority,\n                attempt_count,\n                payload,\n                next_attempt_at,\n                enqueued_at,\n                updated_at\n            FROM delivery_matching_queue\n            WHERE status IN ('queued', 'searching')\n              AND next_attempt_at <= NOW()\n            ORDER BY priority ASC, next_attempt_at ASC\n            LIMIT $1\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"920.751µs","elapsed_secs":0.000920751},"target":"sqlx::query"}

2025-11-28T17:07:53.643295108Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:07:53.643055Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT COUNT(*)::bigint AS count …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT COUNT(*)::bigint AS count\n        FROM video_generation_jobs\n        WHERE status = 'failed'\n          AND updated_at >= NOW() - INTERVAL '24 hours'\n        \n","rows_affected":1,"rows_returned":1,"elapsed":"1.100805ms","elapsed_secs":0.001100805},"target":"sqlx::query"}

2025-11-28T17:07:53.646165674Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:07:53.645923Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT MAX(updated_at) AS last_completed …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT MAX(updated_at) AS last_completed\n        FROM video_generation_jobs\n        WHERE status = 'completed'\n        \n","rows_affected":1,"rows_returned":1,"elapsed":"718.936µs","elapsed_secs":0.000718936},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:07:53.647408963Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:07:53.647313Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT job_id, status, updated_at …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT job_id, status, updated_at\n        FROM video_generation_jobs\n        WHERE status IN ('queued', 'running')\n          AND updated_at < NOW() - INTERVAL '30 minutes'\n        ORDER BY updated_at ASC\n        LIMIT 10\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"652.865µs","elapsed_secs":0.000652865},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:07:53.648989059Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:07:53.648868Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT COUNT(*) AS total …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT COUNT(*) AS total\n        FROM media\n        WHERE media_type = 'video'\n          AND uploaded_at >= NOW() - ($1::int * INTERVAL '1 day')\n        \n","rows_affected":1,"rows_returned":1,"elapsed":"846.969µs","elapsed_secs":0.000846969},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:07:53.650445322Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:07:53.650365Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT\n            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE event_type = 'view')   AS views,\n            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE event_type = 'share')  AS shares,\n            COALESCE(AVG((metadata ->> 'quality_score')::float), 0.0) AS avg_quality\n        FROM media_engagement\n        WHERE occurred_at >= NOW() - ($1::int * INTERVAL '1 day')\n          AND event_type IN ('view', 'share', 'quality_score')\n        \n","rows_affected":1,"rows_returned":1,"elapsed":"786.769µs","elapsed_secs":0.000786769},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:07:53.651873915Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:07:53.651803Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT\n            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE status = 'completed')                       AS completed,\n            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE status IN ('scheduled', 'processing'))      AS pending\n        FROM media_distribution\n        WHERE updated_at >= NOW() - ($1::int * INTERVAL '1 day')\n        \n","rows_affected":1,"rows_returned":1,"elapsed":"738.817µs","elapsed_secs":0.000738817},"target":"sqlx::query"}

2025-11-28T17:08:08.651875007Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:08:08.651722Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT status, COUNT(*)::bigint AS …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT status, COUNT(*)::bigint AS count\n        FROM video_generation_jobs\n        GROUP BY status\n        \n","rows_affected":1,"rows_returned":1,"elapsed":"712.237µs","elapsed_secs":0.000712237},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:08:08.653217437Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:08:08.653130Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT COUNT(*)::bigint AS count …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT COUNT(*)::bigint AS count\n        FROM video_generation_jobs\n        WHERE status = 'failed'\n          AND updated_at >= NOW() - INTERVAL '24 hours'\n        \n","rows_affected":1,"rows_returned":1,"elapsed":"701.716µs","elapsed_secs":0.000701716},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:08:08.654692651Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:08:08.654467Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT COUNT(*)::bigint AS count …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT COUNT(*)::bigint AS count\n        FROM video_generation_jobs\n        WHERE status = 'completed'\n          AND updated_at >= NOW() - INTERVAL '24 hours'\n        \n","rows_affected":1,"rows_returned":1,"elapsed":"666.365µs","elapsed_secs":0.000666365},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:08:08.65591966Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:08:08.655840Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT MAX(updated_at) AS last_completed …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT MAX(updated_at) AS last_completed\n        FROM video_generation_jobs\n        WHERE status = 'completed'\n        \n","rows_affected":1,"rows_returned":1,"elapsed":"706.177µs","elapsed_secs":0.000706177},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:08:08.657287741Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:08:08.657206Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT job_id, status, updated_at …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT job_id, status, updated_at\n        FROM video_generation_jobs\n        WHERE status IN ('queued', 'running')\n          AND updated_at < NOW() - INTERVAL '30 minutes'\n        ORDER BY updated_at ASC\n        LIMIT 10\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"681.895µs","elapsed_secs":0.000681895},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:08:08.658850327Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:08:08.658766Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT COUNT(*) AS total …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT COUNT(*) AS total\n        FROM media\n        WHERE media_type = 'video'\n          AND uploaded_at >= NOW() - ($1::int * INTERVAL '1 day')\n        \n","rows_affected":1,"rows_returned":1,"elapsed":"882.62µs","elapsed_secs":0.00088262},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:08:08.660316191Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:08:08.660229Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT\n            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE event_type = 'view')   AS views,\n            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE event_type = 'share')  AS shares,\n            COALESCE(AVG((metadata ->> 'quality_score')::float), 0.0) AS avg_quality\n        FROM media_engagement\n        WHERE occurred_at >= NOW() - ($1::int * INTERVAL '1 day')\n          AND event_type IN ('view', 'share', 'quality_score')\n        \n","rows_affected":1,"rows_returned":1,"elapsed":"744.017µs","elapsed_secs":0.000744017},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:08:08.661758414Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:08:08.661677Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT\n            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE status = 'completed')                       AS completed,\n            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE status IN ('scheduled', 'processing'))      AS pending\n        FROM media_distribution\n        WHERE updated_at >= NOW() - ($1::int * INTERVAL '1 day')\n        \n","rows_affected":1,"rows_returned":1,"elapsed":"775.938µs","elapsed_secs":0.000775938},"target":"sqlx::query"}

2025-11-28T17:08:19.274061145Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:08:19.273936Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT lfs.id, lfs.live_session_id, lfs.service_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT\n                lfs.id,\n                lfs.live_session_id,\n                lfs.service_id,\n                lfs.promo_price_cfa,\n                lfs.start_at,\n                lfs.scheduled_notification_sent_at,\n                ls.host_user_id,\n                ls.service_id AS primary_service_id,\n                ls.metadata,\n                ls.title\n            FROM live_flash_sales lfs\n            JOIN live_sessions ls ON ls.id = lfs.live_session_id\n            WHERE lfs.status = 'scheduled'\n              AND lfs.start_at <= $1\n              AND lfs.scheduled_notification_sent_at IS NULL\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"1.025753ms","elapsed_secs":0.001025753},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:08:19.275216992Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:08:19.275127Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT id, display_name FROM …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT id, display_name\n        FROM global_promo_events\n        WHERE status IN ('scheduled','live') AND ends_at <= $1\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"757.947µs","elapsed_secs":0.000757947},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:08:19.275516359Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:08:19.275442Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT lfs.id, lfs.live_session_id, lfs.service_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT\n                lfs.id,\n                lfs.live_session_id,\n                lfs.service_id,\n                lfs.promo_price_cfa,\n                ls.host_user_id,\n                ls.service_id AS primary_service_id,\n                ls.metadata,\n                ls.title\n            FROM live_flash_sales lfs\n            JOIN live_sessions ls ON ls.id = lfs.live_session_id\n            WHERE lfs.status = 'scheduled'\n              AND lfs.start_at <= $1\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"821.829µs","elapsed_secs":0.000821829},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:08:19.27644956Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:08:19.276374Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT e.id AS entry_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT\n            e.id AS entry_id,\n            e.service_id,\n            e.submitted_by_user_id,\n            ev.display_name AS event_display_name\n        FROM global_promo_entries e\n        JOIN global_promo_events ev ON ev.id = e.event_id\n        WHERE ev.status = 'live'\n          AND e.status = 'approved'\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"620.835µs","elapsed_secs":0.000620835},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:08:19.277017553Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:08:19.276940Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT lfs.id, lfs.live_session_id, lfs.service_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT\n                lfs.id,\n                lfs.live_session_id,\n                lfs.service_id,\n                lfs.end_at,\n                lfs.ending_notification_sent_at,\n                ls.host_user_id,\n                ls.service_id AS primary_service_id,\n                ls.metadata,\n                ls.title\n            FROM live_flash_sales lfs\n            JOIN live_sessions ls ON ls.id = lfs.live_session_id\n            WHERE lfs.status = 'live'\n              AND lfs.end_at <= $1\n              AND lfs.ending_notification_sent_at IS NULL\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"859.95µs","elapsed_secs":0.00085995},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:08:19.277851493Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:08:19.277754Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT e.id AS entry_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT\n            e.id AS entry_id,\n            e.service_id,\n            e.submitted_by_user_id,\n            ev.display_name AS event_display_name\n        FROM global_promo_entries e\n        JOIN global_promo_events ev ON ev.id = e.event_id\n        WHERE ev.status = 'archived'\n          AND e.status <> 'ended'\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"650.655µs","elapsed_secs":0.000650655},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:08:19.278864356Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:08:19.278762Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"WITH reservations AS ( …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            WITH reservations AS (\n                SELECT flash_sale_id, SUM(quantity) AS reserved_quantity\n                FROM live_flash_sale_reservations\n                GROUP BY flash_sale_id\n            )\n            SELECT\n                lfs.id,\n                lfs.live_session_id,\n                lfs.service_id,\n                lfs.stock_target,\n                COALESCE(res.reserved_quantity, 0) AS reserved_quantity,\n                lfs.commentary_interval_seconds,\n                lfs.last_commentary_sent_at,\n                lfs.end_at,\n                COALESCE(lfs.metadata, '{}'::jsonb) AS flash_metadata,\n                ls.host_user_id,\n                ls.service_id AS primary_service_id,\n                COALESCE(ls.metadata, '{}'::jsonb) AS session_metadata,\n                ls.title\n            FROM live_flash_sales lfs\n            JOIN live_sessions ls ON ls.id = lfs.live_session_id\n            LEFT JOIN reservations res ON res.flash_sale_id = lfs.id\n            WHERE lfs.status = 'live'\n              AND lfs.commentary_mode = 'ai_voice'\n              AND lfs.end_at > $1\n              AND (\n                    lfs.last_commentary_sent_at IS NULL\n                    OR lfs.last_commentary_sent_at <= $1 - make_interval(secs => lfs.commentary_interval_seconds)\n              )\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"1.088806ms","elapsed_secs":0.001088806},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:08:19.281029916Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:08:19.280948Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"UPDATE live_flash_sales SET status …","db.statement":"\n\nUPDATE live_flash_sales SET status = 'ended', updated_at = NOW() WHERE status IN ('scheduled', 'live') AND end_at <= $1\n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"1.524335ms","elapsed_secs":0.001524335},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:08:19.385500732Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:08:19.385363Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT oid::regtype::text, typtype, typcategory, …","db.statement":"\n\nSELECT oid::regtype::text, typtype, typcategory, typrelid, typelem, typbasetype FROM pg_catalog.pg_type WHERE oid = $1\n","rows_affected":1,"rows_returned":1,"elapsed":"1.588686ms","elapsed_secs":0.001588686},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:08:19.387195161Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:08:19.387099Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT enumlabel FROM pg_catalog.pg_enum …","db.statement":"\n\n\nSELECT enumlabel\nFROM pg_catalog.pg_enum\nWHERE enumtypid = $1\nORDER BY enumsortorder\n            \n","rows_affected":9,"rows_returned":9,"elapsed":"1.688158ms","elapsed_secs":0.001688158},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:08:19.388367298Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:08:19.388261Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT id, delivery_id, zone_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT\n                id,\n                delivery_id,\n                zone_id,\n                status,\n                priority,\n                attempt_count,\n                payload,\n                next_attempt_at,\n                enqueued_at,\n                updated_at\n            FROM delivery_matching_queue\n            WHERE status IN ('queued', 'searching')\n              AND next_attempt_at <= NOW()\n            ORDER BY priority ASC, next_attempt_at ASC\n            LIMIT $1\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"5.63532ms","elapsed_secs":0.00563532},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:08:19.388401038Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:08:19.388291Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"message":"[DeliveryMatchingWorker] Aucune livraison à traiter (batch = 10)","log.target":"yukpomnang_backend::tasks::delivery_matching_worker","log.module_path":"yukpomnang_backend::tasks::delivery_matching_worker","log.file":"src/tasks/delivery_matching_worker.rs","log.line":61},"target":"yukpomnang_backend::tasks::delivery_matching_worker"}
2025-11-28T17:08:19.474668065Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:08:19.474390Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT id, media_id, platform, …","db.statement":"\n\nSELECT id,\n                 media_id,\n                 platform,\n                 payload,\n                 status,\n                 attempt,\n                 last_error,\n                 scheduled_for,\n                 created_at,\n                 updated_at\n         FROM social_publication_jobs\n         WHERE status = 'queued' AND scheduled_for <= NOW()\n         ORDER BY scheduled_for ASC\n         LIMIT $1\n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"2.20441ms","elapsed_secs":0.00220441},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:08:19.487836578Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:08:19.487673Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT delivery_id, suggested_status, created_at, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT \n            delivery_id,\n            suggested_status,\n            created_at,\n            auto_confirm_after_seconds\n        FROM delivery_proximity_suggestions\n        WHERE \n            status = 'pending'\n            AND auto_confirm_after_seconds IS NOT NULL\n            AND created_at + (auto_confirm_after_seconds || ' seconds')::interval <= NOW()\n        LIMIT 50\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"709.286µs","elapsed_secs":0.000709286},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:08:19.488238248Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:08:19.488114Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT id, service_id, product_index, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT \n            id,\n            service_id,\n            product_index,\n            client_user_id,\n            provider_user_id\n        FROM product_orders\n        WHERE \n            status = 'pending'\n            AND validation_deadline IS NOT NULL\n            AND validation_deadline <= $1\n        LIMIT 50\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"1.241499ms","elapsed_secs":0.001241499},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:08:19.4913879Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:08:19.491117Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT d.id, d.status::text as …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT \n                d.id,\n                d.status::text as status,\n                d.updated_at,\n                d.creator_id,\n                d.recipient_user_id,\n                c.user_id as courier_user_id\n            FROM deliveries d\n            LEFT JOIN couriers c ON c.id = d.courier_id\n            WHERE \n                d.status::text = $1\n                AND d.updated_at < NOW() - ($2 || ' minutes')::interval\n                AND d.status::text != 'delivered'\n                AND d.status::text != 'cancelled'\n                AND d.status::text != 'completed'\n            LIMIT 20\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"2.384635ms","elapsed_secs":0.002384635},"target":"sqlx::query"}

2025-11-28T17:08:49.277700216Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:08:49.277628Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT lfs.id, lfs.live_session_id, lfs.service_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT\n                lfs.id,\n                lfs.live_session_id,\n                lfs.service_id,\n                lfs.end_at,\n                lfs.ending_notification_sent_at,\n                ls.host_user_id,\n                ls.service_id AS primary_service_id,\n                ls.metadata,\n                ls.title\n            FROM live_flash_sales lfs\n            JOIN live_sessions ls ON ls.id = lfs.live_session_id\n            WHERE lfs.status = 'live'\n              AND lfs.end_at <= $1\n              AND lfs.ending_notification_sent_at IS NULL\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"829.289µs","elapsed_secs":0.000829289},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:08:49.278008203Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:08:49.277891Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT e.id AS entry_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT\n            e.id AS entry_id,\n            e.service_id,\n            e.submitted_by_user_id,\n            ev.display_name AS event_display_name\n        FROM global_promo_entries e\n        JOIN global_promo_events ev ON ev.id = e.event_id\n        WHERE ev.status = 'live'\n          AND e.status = 'approved'\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"662.225µs","elapsed_secs":0.000662225},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:08:49.27920804Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:08:49.279115Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT e.id AS entry_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT\n            e.id AS entry_id,\n            e.service_id,\n            e.submitted_by_user_id,\n            ev.display_name AS event_display_name\n        FROM global_promo_entries e\n        JOIN global_promo_events ev ON ev.id = e.event_id\n        WHERE ev.status = 'archived'\n          AND e.status <> 'ended'\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"614.714µs","elapsed_secs":0.000614714},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:08:49.279559538Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:08:49.279485Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"WITH reservations AS ( …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            WITH reservations AS (\n                SELECT flash_sale_id, SUM(quantity) AS reserved_quantity\n                FROM live_flash_sale_reservations\n                GROUP BY flash_sale_id\n            )\n            SELECT\n                lfs.id,\n                lfs.live_session_id,\n                lfs.service_id,\n                lfs.stock_target,\n                COALESCE(res.reserved_quantity, 0) AS reserved_quantity,\n                lfs.commentary_interval_seconds,\n                lfs.last_commentary_sent_at,\n                lfs.end_at,\n                COALESCE(lfs.metadata, '{}'::jsonb) AS flash_metadata,\n                ls.host_user_id,\n                ls.service_id AS primary_service_id,\n                COALESCE(ls.metadata, '{}'::jsonb) AS session_metadata,\n                ls.title\n            FROM live_flash_sales lfs\n            JOIN live_sessions ls ON ls.id = lfs.live_session_id\n            LEFT JOIN reservations res ON res.flash_sale_id = lfs.id\n            WHERE lfs.status = 'live'\n              AND lfs.commentary_mode = 'ai_voice'\n              AND lfs.end_at > $1\n              AND (\n                    lfs.last_commentary_sent_at IS NULL\n                    OR lfs.last_commentary_sent_at <= $1 - make_interval(secs => lfs.commentary_interval_seconds)\n              )\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"1.154286ms","elapsed_secs":0.001154286},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:08:49.281057733Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:08:49.280977Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"UPDATE live_flash_sales SET status …","db.statement":"\n\nUPDATE live_flash_sales SET status = 'ended', updated_at = NOW() WHERE status IN ('scheduled', 'live') AND end_at <= $1\n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"822.518µs","elapsed_secs":0.000822518},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:08:49.385117089Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:08:49.382709Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT id, delivery_id, zone_id, …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT\n                id,\n                delivery_id,\n                zone_id,\n                status,\n                priority,\n                attempt_count,\n                payload,\n                next_attempt_at,\n                enqueued_at,\n                updated_at\n            FROM delivery_matching_queue\n            WHERE status IN ('queued', 'searching')\n              AND next_attempt_at <= NOW()\n            ORDER BY priority ASC, next_attempt_at ASC\n            LIMIT $1\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"972.483µs","elapsed_secs":0.000972483},"target":"sqlx::query"}

2025-11-28T17:08:53.650192426Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:08:53.650085Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT job_id, status, updated_at …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT job_id, status, updated_at\n        FROM video_generation_jobs\n        WHERE status IN ('queued', 'running')\n          AND updated_at < NOW() - INTERVAL '30 minutes'\n        ORDER BY updated_at ASC\n        LIMIT 10\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"695.886µs","elapsed_secs":0.000695886},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:08:53.652024058Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:08:53.651763Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT COUNT(*) AS total …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT COUNT(*) AS total\n        FROM media\n        WHERE media_type = 'video'\n          AND uploaded_at >= NOW() - ($1::int * INTERVAL '1 day')\n        \n","rows_affected":1,"rows_returned":1,"elapsed":"1.006933ms","elapsed_secs":0.001006933},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:08:53.656201954Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:08:53.656063Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT\n            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE event_type = 'view')   AS views,\n            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE event_type = 'share')  AS shares,\n            COALESCE(AVG((metadata ->> 'quality_score')::float), 0.0) AS avg_quality\n        FROM media_engagement\n        WHERE occurred_at >= NOW() - ($1::int * INTERVAL '1 day')\n          AND event_type IN ('view', 'share', 'quality_score')\n        \n","rows_affected":1,"rows_returned":1,"elapsed":"963.392µs","elapsed_secs":0.000963392},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:08:53.657796421Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:08:53.657693Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT\n            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE status = 'completed')                       AS completed,\n            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE status IN ('scheduled', 'processing'))      AS pending\n        FROM media_distribution\n        WHERE updated_at >= NOW() - ($1::int * INTERVAL '1 day')\n        \n","rows_affected":1,"rows_returned":1,"elapsed":"804.828µs","elapsed_secs":0.000804828},"target":"sqlx::query"}

2025-11-28T17:09:03.049116857Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:09:03.048988Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT d.id FROM deliveries …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT d.id\n            FROM deliveries d\n            LEFT JOIN couriers c ON d.courier_id = c.id\n            WHERE\n                (\n                    d.creator_id = $1\n                    OR d.recipient_user_id = $1\n                    OR c.user_id = $1\n                )\n                AND d.status <> 'completed'::delivery_status\n            ORDER BY d.requested_at DESC\n            LIMIT $2\n            \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"1.948334ms","elapsed_secs":0.001948334},"target":"sqlx::query"}

2025-11-28T17:09:08.643130618Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:09:08.643026Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT status, COUNT(*)::bigint AS …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT status, COUNT(*)::bigint AS count\n        FROM video_generation_jobs\n        GROUP BY status\n        \n","rows_affected":1,"rows_returned":1,"elapsed":"931.671µs","elapsed_secs":0.000931671},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:09:08.644907019Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:09:08.644663Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT COUNT(*)::bigint AS count …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT COUNT(*)::bigint AS count\n        FROM video_generation_jobs\n        WHERE status = 'failed'\n          AND updated_at >= NOW() - INTERVAL '24 hours'\n        \n","rows_affected":1,"rows_returned":1,"elapsed":"849.03µs","elapsed_secs":0.00084903},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:09:08.646749651Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:09:08.646546Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT COUNT(*)::bigint AS count …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT COUNT(*)::bigint AS count\n        FROM video_generation_jobs\n        WHERE status = 'completed'\n          AND updated_at >= NOW() - INTERVAL '24 hours'\n        \n","rows_affected":1,"rows_returned":1,"elapsed":"881.08µs","elapsed_secs":0.00088108},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:09:08.648742197Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:09:08.648435Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT MAX(updated_at) AS last_completed …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT MAX(updated_at) AS last_completed\n        FROM video_generation_jobs\n        WHERE status = 'completed'\n        \n","rows_affected":1,"rows_returned":1,"elapsed":"984.103µs","elapsed_secs":0.000984103},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:09:08.651617903Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:09:08.651406Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT job_id, status, updated_at …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT job_id, status, updated_at\n        FROM video_generation_jobs\n        WHERE status IN ('queued', 'running')\n          AND updated_at < NOW() - INTERVAL '30 minutes'\n        ORDER BY updated_at ASC\n        LIMIT 10\n        \n","rows_affected":0,"rows_returned":0,"elapsed":"2.17007ms","elapsed_secs":0.00217007},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:09:08.654476889Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:09:08.654373Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT COUNT(*) AS total …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT COUNT(*) AS total\n        FROM media\n        WHERE media_type = 'video'\n          AND uploaded_at >= NOW() - ($1::int * INTERVAL '1 day')\n        \n","rows_affected":1,"rows_returned":1,"elapsed":"854.919µs","elapsed_secs":0.000854919},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:09:08.656183718Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:09:08.655869Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT\n            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE event_type = 'view')   AS views,\n            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE event_type = 'share')  AS shares,\n            COALESCE(AVG((metadata ->> 'quality_score')::float), 0.0) AS avg_quality\n        FROM media_engagement\n        WHERE occurred_at >= NOW() - ($1::int * INTERVAL '1 day')\n          AND event_type IN ('view', 'share', 'quality_score')\n        \n","rows_affected":1,"rows_returned":1,"elapsed":"751.557µs","elapsed_secs":0.000751557},"target":"sqlx::query"}
2025-11-28T17:09:08.658305777Z {"timestamp":"2025-11-28T17:09:08.658215Z","level":"DEBUG","fields":{"summary":"SELECT COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE …","db.statement":"\n\n\n        SELECT\n            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE status = 'completed')                       AS completed,\n            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE status IN ('scheduled', 'processing'))      AS pending\n        FROM media_distribution\n        WHERE updated_at >= NOW() - ($1::int * INTERVAL '1 day')\n        \n","rows_affected":1,"rows_returned":1,"elapsed":"1.605757ms","elapsed_secs":0.001605757},"target":"sqlx::query"}
